##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यु॒ष्माकं॑ बु॒द्धे अ॒पां न याम॑नि विधु॒र्यति॒ न म॒ही  
> श्र॑थ॒र्यति॑ ।  
> वि॒श्वप्सु॑र्य॒ज्ञो अ॒र्वाग॒यं सु वः॒ प्रय॑स्वंतो॒ न स॒त्राच॒  ॑
> आ गत ॥ ४ ॥

### Printed p. 135

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> युष्माकं । बुद्धे । अपां । न । यामनि विदुर्यति । न । मही । श्रथर्यति ।  
> विश्वप्सुः । यज्ञः अर्वाक् । अयं । सु । वः । प्रयस्वंतः । न । सत्राचः । आ ।  
> गत ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे मरुतः युष्माकं बुद्धे परस्परसंघातेऽपां न यामनि प्रवृद्धानामुदकानां गमन इव मही महती भूर्न विधुर्यति । न व्यथते । नापि श्रथर्यति ।
> न विशीर्णा भवति । शीघ्रगतयोऽपि यूयमेनां न पीडयध्वमित्यर्थः ।
> विश्वप्सुर्विश्वरूपोऽयं यज्ञो यागसाधनं हविर्वो युष्माकमर्वागभिमुखं
> सु सुष्टु गच्छति । प्रयस्वंतो नान्नवंतः परिष्कर्तार इव सुखप्रदाः संतः सत्राचः
> सहांचना आ गत । आगच्छत । संघाकारेणागच्छतेत्यर्थः । सप्तगणा वै मरुतः ।
> तै सं. २-२-५-७ । इति श्रुतेः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Maruts, in your mutual collision, as at the going of swollen waters, the great earth does not tremble, nor is it shattered; that is, though you move swiftly you do not oppress it. This manifold sacrifice, the oblation, the means of sacrifice, goes well towards you. Come, giving pleasure like those who have food, together; that is, come in the form of a host, for there is the Vedic text सप्तगणा वै मरुतः (*saptagaṇā vai marutaḥ*) (Tai. Saṃ. 2-2-5-7).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अपां (*apāṃ*)—of masses of water
- यामनि न (*yāmani na*)—as at the time of a flood (O Maruts)
- युष्माकं (*yuṣmākaṃ*)—your
- बुध्ने (*budhne*)—even when the host moves
- मही (*mahī*)—the great earth
- न विधुरति (*na vidhurati*)—does not move
- न श्रथरति (*na śratharati*)—does not become blunted
- विश्वप्सुः (*viśvapsuḥ*)—having many kinds of forms
- अयं यज्ञः (*ayaṃ yajñaḥ*)—this sacrifice
- अर्वाक् (*arvāk*)—towards you
- सु (*su*)—comes exceedingly
- प्रयस्वंतो न (*prayasvaṃto na*)—like men filled with food (giving pleasure)
- सत्राचः (*satrācaḥ*)—gathered together
- आ गत (*ā gata*)—come

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Maruts, as, even when masses of water flow, the earth neither moves nor is shattered, so, though your host moves, the earth does not tremble. This sacrifice, which has many forms, approaches you. Come together, giving pleasure like men filled with food.

##### English translation
In your collision, as in the transit of waters, the earth is not shaken, is not shattered; this manifold sacrifice comes towards you; approach united as if laden with food.

### Printed p. 136

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यू॒यं धू॒र्षु प्र॒युजो॒ न र॒श्मिभि॒र्ज्योति॑ष्मंतो॒ न ॒ ॑
> भा॒सा व्यु॑ष्टिषु ।  
> शै॒नासो॒ न स्वय॑शसो रि॒शाद॑सः प्र॒वासो॒ न प्रसि॑तासः परि॒पुषः॑ ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यूयं । धूःसु । प्रयुजः । न । रश्मिभिः । जोतिष्मंतः । न । भासा । विउष्टिषु ।  
> श्वेनासः । न । स्वयशसः । रिशादसः । प्रवासः । न । प्रसितासः । परिपुषः ॥ ४ ॥

**NOTE**

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे मरुतः यूयं धूर्षु रथसंबंधिनीषु रश्मिभिर्योक्रैः प्रयुक्ता बद्धा अश्वा इव परिप्रुषः परितो गंतारः स्थ । तथा ज्योतिष्ठंतो न तेजोवंत आदित्यादय इव भासा युक्ता भवथ व्युष्टिषूषःसूदितासु । किंच श्येनासो न श्येना इव स्वयशसः स्वायत्तयशसः ते यथा रिशादसो रिशताम- सितारस्तद्वद्रितादसः स्वायत्तयशसश्च । किंच प्रवासो न प्रवासिन इव पथिका इव प्रसिद्धयानाः । उक्तरूपा मरुतः परिप्रुषः परितो गंतारो भवथ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Maruts, you are like horses yoked with reins to the chariot-poles, going about on every side. Likewise, like luminous beings such as the sun, you are endowed with radiance at the dawns. Further, like hawks, you have fame dependent on yourselves; as they are eaters of flesh and have fame of their own, so are you destroyers and have fame of your own. Further, like travellers, like wayfarers, you have well-known means of going. Such Maruts, you are going all around. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—O Maruts
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- रश्मिभिः (*raśmibhiḥ*)—with reins
- धूर्षु (*dhūrṣu*)—to the chariot-poles
- प्रयुजः न (*prayujaḥ na*)—like yoked horses
- परिपुषः (*paripuṣaḥ*)—you move about
- व्यष्टिषु (*vyaṣṭiṣu*)—at the times of dawn
- जोतिष्मंतो न (*jotiṣmaṃto na*)—like the radiant Ādityas
- भासा (*bhāsā*)—you are endowed with brightness
- शैनासो न (*śaināso na*)—like hawks
- स्वयशसः (*svayaśasaḥ*)—having fame acquired by yourselves
- रिशाससः (*riśāsasaḥ*)—you are destroyers of enemies
- प्रवासो न (*pravāso na*)—like travellers
- प्रसितासः (*prasitāsaḥ*)—having excellent movement

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Maruts, you move about like horses yoked with reins to the chariot-poles. At the time of dawn you are endowed with brightness like the radiant Ādityas. Like hawks,

### Printed p. 137

you have fame acquired by yourselves and are destroyers of enemies. Like travellers you have excellent movement.

##### English translation
You (move along) like horses harnessed to the chariot-poles with reins; at break of day you (are endowed) with radiance like the luminaries (of heaven); you are like hawks, like the destroyers of foes, desirous of renown; you cover the ground like travellers, you move everywhere.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र यद्वह॑ध्व मरुतः परा॒काद्य॒यं म॒हः सं॒वर॑णस्य॒ वस्वः॑ ।  
> वि॒दा॒नासो॑ वसवो॒ राध्य॑स्या॒राच्चि॒द्देषः॑ सनु॒तर्यु॑योत ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । यत् । वहध्व । मरुतः । पराकात् । यूयं । महः । संवरणस्य । वस्वः ।  
> विदानासः । वसवः । राध्यस्य । आरात् । चित् । द्वेषः । सनुतः । युयोत ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे मरुतः यूयं यद्यदा पराकादत्यंतं दूरदेशाद्वहध्वे आगच्छथ
> तदानीं महो महत्संवरणस्य संवरणीयं राध्यस्य संराधनीयं वसो वसु
> धनं विवानासः प्रयच्छंतो हे वसवः यूयमाराच्चिद्दूरादेव युयोत ।
> पृथक्कुरुत । कानिति उच्यते । द्वेषो द्वेष्टवान् सनुतरंतर्हितान् निगूढानित्यर्थः ।
> सनुतरित्यंतर्हितनाम ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Maruts, when you come from a very distant place, then, giving great, desirable, accomplishable wealth, O Vasus, you separate from afar the haters, that is, the enemies who are hidden and concealed. He says that the word for "hidden" is a name for the concealed.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—O Maruts
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- यत् (*yat*)—when
- पराकात् (*parākāt*)—from a very distant place
- प्र वहध्वे (*pra vahadhve*)—you come, then
- महः (*mahaḥ*)—great
- संवरणस्य (*saṃvaraṇasya*)—desired
- राध्यस्य (*rādhyasya*)—worthy of honour
- वस्वः (*vasvaḥ*)—wealth
- विदानासः (*vidānāsaḥ*)—giving
- वसवः (*vasavaḥ*)—you who give dwellings
- अराच्चित् (*arāccit*)—from afar itself
- सनुतः (*sanutaḥ*)—hidden, concealed
- द्वेषः (*dveṣaḥ*)—enemies
- युयोत (*yuyota*)—drive away

### Printed p. 138

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Maruts, when you come from a very distant place, then, giving us great, desired and honoured wealth, you who give dwellings drive away from afar our enemies who lie hidden and concealed.

##### English translation
When, Maruts, you come from a distance, then, Vasus, distributors of abundant, desirable, coveted wealth, you scatter afar our concealed foes.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> य उ॒दृचि॑ य॒ज्ञे अ॑ध्वरे॒ष्णा म॒रुद्भो॒ न मानु॑षो॒  ॑
> ददाशत् ।  
> रे॒वत्स वयो॑ दधते सु॒वीरं॒ स दे॒वाना॒मपि॑ गोपी॒थे अ॑स्तु ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । उत्खिचि । यज्ञे । अध्वरेस्थाः । मरुत्भ्यः । न । मानुषः । ददाशत् ।  
> रेवत् । सः । वयः । दधते । सुवीरं । सः । देवानां । अपि । गोपीथे । अस्तु ॥७॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अध्वरेष्ठा यागे सीदन्मानुषो यो यजमानो यज्ञ उदृचि सति ।
> ऋक्कब्बेन स्तोत्रमुपलक्ष्यते । यज्ञे समाप्तस्तुतिके सति संपूर्णे सति मरुद्भो
> न मरुद्भ इवान्येषामध्वर्युभ्य ऋत्विगादिभ्यो ददाशत् दद्यात् । यद्वा ।
> यज्ञ उदृच्युपक्रांत इत्यर्थः । तस्मिन्यज्ञे मरुद्भ इवान्यस्मा इंद्राय ददाशत्
> ददाति । स यजमानो रेवद्धनवत्सुवीरं शोभनपुत्राद्युपेतं वयोऽन्नं दधते ।
> धारयति ॥ व्यत्ययेनैकवचनं ॥ स देवानामिंद्रादीनामपि गोपीथे सोम
> पानेऽस्तु । भवतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sacrificer, seated at the rite, who, when the stotra is completed in the sacrifice (the word Ṛc stands for a stotra), gives to others, to the adhvaryus, priests and the rest, as if to the Maruts. Or else: when the sacrifice has begun, he gives in that sacrifice to another, to Indra, as to the Maruts. That sacrificer holds wealthy food with good heroes, that is, with good sons and the like (the singular is used for the plural by a change of number). May he be present at the Soma-drinking of the gods, Indra and the others.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अध्वरेष्ठाः (*adhvareṣṭhāḥ*)—seated at the sacrifice
- यः मानुषः (*yaḥ mānuṣaḥ*)—the sacrificer who
- यज्ञे (*yajñe*)—in the sacrifice
- उदृचि (*udṛci*)—when the praise at the time of completion has been recited
- मरुद्भो न (*marudbho na*)—as one offers oblation to the Maruts

### Printed p. 139

- ददाशत् (*dadāśat*)—gives wealth to the priests
- सः (*saḥ*)—that same sacrificer
- रेवत् (*revat*)—wealthy
- सुवीरं (*suvīraṃ*)—endowed with heroic sons
- वयः (*vayaḥ*)—food
- पधते (*padhate*)—obtains
- सः (*saḥ*)—he
- देवानां (*devānāṃ*)—of the other gods
- गोपीथे अपि (*gopīthe api*)—at the times of Soma-drinking also
- अस्तु (*astu*)—may he be engaged in rites

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The sacrificer seated at the sacrifice who, when the praise at the time of completion has been recited in the sacrifice, gives wealth to the priests as one offers oblation to the Maruts, that same sacrificer obtains food that is wealthy and endowed with heroic sons. May he be engaged in rites at the times of Soma-drinking of the other gods also.

##### English translation
The worshipper who engaged in worship at the sacrifice, accompanied with praise, presents donations (to the priests) as if to the Maruts, he enjoys food with riches, and male progeny; may he be present at thc god's Soma-drinking.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ते हि य॒ज्ञेषु॑ य॒ज्जिया॑स॒ ऊमा॑ आदि॒त्येन॒ नाम्ना॒ शंभ॑विष्ठाः ।  
> ते नो॑वंतु रथ॒तूर्म॑नी॒षां म॒हश्च॒ याम॑न्नध्व॒रे च॑का॒नाः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ते । हि । यज्ञेषु । यज्जियासः । ऊमाः । आदित्येन । नाम्ना । शंभविष्ठाः ।  
> ते । नः । अवंतु । रथ्यतूः । मनीषां । महः । च । यामन् । अध्वरे । चकानाः॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ते हि ते खलु यज्ञेषु यागेषु यज्ञियासो यज्ञार्हा यष्टव्या
> ऊमा अवितार आदित्येन नाम्नादित्यसंबंधिनोदकेन शंभविष्ठाः सुखस्य भावयितारः ।
> यद्वा । आदित्याख्येन नाम्नादित्यनामकेन देवेन सह शंभविष्ठाः । ते मरुतो

### Printed p. 140

> नोऽस्मानवंतु । रथतू रथतुरो रथस्य यज्ञगमनसाधनस्य त्वरयितारः संतो
> मनीषां स्तुतिमवंतु । रक्षंतु । कीदृशास्ते । अध्वरे यामन्यागगमने महो
> महद्धविश्वकानाः कामयमानाः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

They, at the sacrifices, are worthy of sacrifice, to be worshipped, protectors, bestowers of happiness through the water of the Ādityas, called by the name Āditya. Or else: together with the deity named Āditya they are the greatest bestowers of happiness. May those Maruts protect us; being the hasteners of the chariot, which is the means of going to the sacrifice, may they protect our praise. How are they? At the coming to the sacrifice they are desirous of the great oblation.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ते हि (*te hi*)—those same Maruts
- यज्ञेषु (*yajñeṣu*)—at the times of sacrifice
- यज्ञियासः (*yajñiyāsaḥ*)—worthy of worship
- ऊमाः (*ūmāḥ*)—protectors
- आदित्येन नाम्ना (*ādityena nāmnā*)—together with the water connected with the Āditya
- शंभविष्ठाः (*śaṃbhaviṣṭhāḥ*)—they are bestowers of happiness
- ते (*te*)—those same Maruts
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- अवंतु (*avaṃtu*)—may they protect
- रथतूः (*rathatūḥ*)—those who drive the chariot for the sacrifice
- अध्वरे (*adhvare*)—at the sacrifice
- यामन् (*yāman*)—when the oblation is offered
- महः (*mahaḥ*)—the great oblation
- चकानाः (*cakānāḥ*)—those who desire it, the Maruts
- मनीषां (*manīṣāṃ*)—may they make our praise fruitful

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

At the times of sacrifice the Maruts are worthy of worship, protectors, and bestowers of happiness together with the water connected with the Āditya. May those same Maruts protect us. May those same Maruts, who drive the chariot for the sacrifice and desire the oblation at the time of sacrifice, make our praise fruitful.

##### English translation
May those protecting deities who are entitled to be worshipped at sacrifices, who under the name of Adityas are the bestowers of felicity, may they (the Maruts) accelerating their chariots, and desirous of the ample (oblation), upon their arrival at the sacrifice protect our praise.

## Sūkta 78 (Maṇḍala 10)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> विप्रास इत्यष्टर्चं दशमं सूक्तं । पंचमाद्यास्तिस्रो द्वितीया चेति
> चतस्रो जगत्यः । शिष्टाश्च तसस्त्रिष्टुभः । पूर्ववदृषिदेवते । तथा चानुक्रांतं ।
> विप्रासो द्वितीया पंचमाद्याश्च तिस्रो जगत्य इति ॥ गतो विनियोगः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning विप्रास (*viprāsa*) has eight Ṛks and is the tenth sūkta. The fifth and the two that follow it, and the second, four Ṛks in all, are Jagatīs; the remaining four are Triṣṭubhs. The seer and the deity are as before. So the Anukramaṇī records विप्रासो द्वितीया पंचमाद्याश्च तिस्रो जगत्य (*viprāso dvitīyā paṃcamādyāśca tisro jagatya*). The ritual application has been given.

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta beginning विप्रासः (*viprāsaḥ*) is the tenth sūkta in the sixth anuvāka. It has eight Ṛks. The seer and deities of this sūkta are the same as those of the preceding sūkta. In this sūkta the fifth,

### Printed p. 141

sixth, seventh and second Ṛks are in the Jagatī metre. The remaining four Ṛks are in the Triṣṭubh metre. In the Anukramaṇī it is said विप्रासो द्वितीया पंचमाद्याश्च (*viprāso dvitīyā paṃcamādyāśca*) and the three Jagatīs. The ritual application has been stated earlier.

Maṇḍala 10; Anuvāka 6; Sūkta 78. Aṣṭaka 8; Adhyāya 3; Varga 12, 13. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 8. Seer: स्युमरश्मिर्भार्गवः (*syumaraśmirbhārgavaḥ*). Deity: मरुतः (*marutaḥ*). Metre: Ṛks 1, 3, 4, 8 Triṣṭubh; Ṛks 2, 5-7 Jagatī.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> विप्रा॑सो॒ न मन्म॑भिः स्वा॒ध्यो॑ देवा॒व्यो॒३॒॑ ३ न य॒ज्ञैः स्वप्न॑सः ।  
> राजा॑नो॒ न चि॒त्राः सु॑सं॒दृशः॑ क्षिती॒नां न मर्या॑ अरे॒पसः॑ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> विप्रासः । न । मन्मभिः । सुआध्यः । देवअव्यः । न । यज्ञैः । सुअप्नसः ।  
> राजानः । न । चित्राः । सुसंदृशः । क्षितीनां । न । मर्याः । अरेपसः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> विप्रासो न विप्रा ब्राह्मणा इव ते यथा मन्मभिः स्तुतिभिः स्वाध्यः शोभनाध्याना भवंति एवं मरुतोऽप्यस्मदीयैर्मनभिर्मननीयैः स्तोत्रैः स्वाध्यः
> सुमनसो भवंति । अथवा मन्मभिः स्तोत्रैर्युक्ता विप्रासो न मेधाविनः
> स्तोतार इव स्वाध्यो यज्ञे यजमाने ना सुध्याना इत्यर्थः । तथा यज्ञैर्यागैर्देवाव्यो न देवानां तर्पयितारो ये यजमाना इव स्वप्नसः । अप्प
> इति कर्मनाम । शोभनकर्माणः । ते यथा कर्मसु व्यापृता भवंति तद्वदृष्टिप्रदानादिकर्मसु व्यापृता भवंति । राजानो न मूर्धाभिषिक्ताः क्षितिपतय इव
> चित्राश्चायनीयाः पूजनीयाः । यद्वा चित्राभरणाः । तथा सुसंदृशः सुष्टु
> दर्शनीयाः । तथा क्षितीनां निवासानां स्वामिनो मर्या न मनुष्या इवारेप-

### Printed p. 142

> सोऽपापाः । यथा प्रतिग्रहार्थमन्यत्रागत्वा स्वगृह एवानुतिष्ठंतो निर्दोषा
> भवंति तादृशा इत्यर्थः । ईदृशाः शोभंत इत्यर्थः ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Like priests, like Brahmans, as they become good in meditation through praises, so the Maruts also become well-minded through our praises, which are to be pondered. Or else: like wise praisers endowed with praises, good in meditation, so the Maruts are well-meditating towards the sacrificer at the sacrifice. **NOTE** Likewise, with sacrifices, like the sacrificers who gratify the gods, they are of good works; as those are busy in their rites, so these are busy in works such as the giving of rain. Like kings, like anointed lords of the earth, they are to be gazed at and worshipped; or else they have variegated ornaments. Likewise they are well to look upon. Likewise, like men who are lords of dwellings, they are sinless. As those who do not go elsewhere for gifts but perform the rites in their own house are free from fault, such are they; that is, they shine in this manner.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—the Maruts
- विप्रासो न (*viprāso na*)—like wise sages
- मन्मभि: (*manmabhi:*)—together with praises
- स्वाध्यः (*svādhyaḥ*)—they are good in meditation
- यज्ञैः (*yajñaiḥ*)—by sacrifices
- देवाव्यो न (*devāvyo na*)—like sacrificers who gratify the gods
- स्वप्नसः (*svapnasaḥ*)—they are of good works
- राजानो न (*rājāno na*)—like kings
- चित्रा: (*citrā:*)—having variegated ornaments
- सुसंदृशः (*susaṃdṛśaḥ*)—having an attractive form
- क्षितीनां (*kṣitīnāṃ*)—who are lords of dwellings
- मर्या: न (*maryā: na*)—like men
- अरेपस: (*arepasa:*)—they are free from sin

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The Maruts are good in meditation, endowed with praises, like wise sages. Like sacrificers who gratify the gods by sacrifices, they are of good works. Like kings, they have variegated ornaments and an attractive form. Like men who are lords of dwellings, they are free from sin.

##### English translation
They (the Maruts) are like Brahmans sanctified by pious praises, they are institutors of holy rites like the propitiators of the gods by sacrifices; decked with various ornaments, and handsome like kings, devoid of sin like men (who are the lords) of habitations.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निर्न ये भ्राज॑सा रु॒क्मव॑क्षसो॒ वाता॑सो॒ न स्व॒युजः॑ स॒द्यऊ॑तयः !  
> प्र॒ज्ञा॒तारो॒ न ज्येष्ठाः॑ सुनी॒तयः॑ सु॒शर्मा॑णो॒ न सोमा॑ ॒ ॒
> ऋतं यते ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 143

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निः । न । ये । भ्राजसा । रुक्ष्मवक्षसः । वातासः । न । स्वयुजः । सद्यःऊतयः।  
> पृज्ञातारः । न । ज्येष्ठाः । सुनीतयः । सुशर्माणः । न । सोमाः । ऋतं ।  
> यते ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ये मरुतोऽग्निर्नाग्निरिव स यथा भ्राजसा तेजसा शोभते तद्वद्भाजसा
> युक्ताः किंच रुक्मवक्षसो रुक्मालंकृतवक्षस्का वातासो न प्रत्यक्षवाता इव स्वयुजः
> स्वयंयुज्यमानाः सद्यऊतयः सद्योगमनाः प्रज्ञातारो न प्रकर्षेण ज्ञातारो
> ज्ञानिन इव ज्येष्णाः पूज्याः सुनीतयः सुनयनाः सुशर्माणो न सोमाः सुसुखाः
> सोमा इव ते यूयमृतं यते यज्ञं गच्छते यजमानाय गच्छतेति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Maruts who, like Agni, shine with splendour, are endowed with splendour in the same way. Further, they have breasts adorned with golden ornaments; like visibly blowing winds they yoke themselves of their own accord; they are swift-going, quick in their going; like those who know in a high degree, that is, like the learned, they are venerable; they are good leaders; like the Somas they are very pleasant. Such Maruts, you go to the sacrifice, you go to the sacrificer.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्निर्न (*agnirna*)—like Agni
- भ्राजसा (*bhrājasā*)—endowed with splendour
- रुक्ष्मवक्षसः (*rukṣmavakṣasaḥ*)—having breasts with golden ornaments
- वातासो न (*vātāso na*)—like winds
- स्वयुजः (*svayujaḥ*)—self-impelled
- सद्यऊतयः (*sadyaūtayaḥ*)—swift protectors
- प्रज्ञातारो न (*prajñātāro na*)—like the learned
- ज्येष्ठाः (*jyeṣṭhāḥ*)—venerable
- सुनीतयः (*sunītayaḥ*)—excellent guides
- सुशर्माणः (*suśarmāṇaḥ*)—who give excellent happiness
- सोमाः न (*somāḥ na*)—like the Somas, you
- ऋतं यते (*ṛtaṃ yate*)—come to the sacrificer who performs the sacrifice

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Maruts, you who are endowed with splendour like Agni, whose breasts bear golden ornaments, who are self-impelled like winds, swift protectors, venerable and guides like the learned, and givers of excellent happiness like the Somas, come to the sacrificer who performs the sacrifice.

##### English translation
(You) who like Agni (are endowed) with splendour, who have golden ornaments upon your breasts, who like the winds (are) self-yoked, swift-moving, who like the extremely wise (are) venerable and able guides, and like the Soma-juices the givers of delight, (you come) to the (worshipper) going to the sacrifice.

### Printed p. 144

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

For the sentence in this Ṛk अग्निर्नये भ्राजसा रुक्मवक्षसः (*agnirnaye bhrājasā rukmavakṣasaḥ*), Yāska gives the explanation अग्निरिव ये मरुतो भ्राजमाना रोचिष्टूरस्का भ्राजस्वंतो रुक्मवक्षसः (*agniriva ye maruto bhrājamānā rociṣṭūraskā bhrājasvaṃto rukmavakṣasaḥ*) (Ni. 3-15); that is, the Marut deities shine like Agni, and blaze with the shining golden ornaments on their breasts and with exceeding radiance.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वाता॑सो॒ न ये धुन॑यो जिग॒त्नवो॑ग्नी॒नां न जि॒ह्वा वि॑रो॒किणः॑ ।  
> वर्म॑ण्वंतो॒ न यो॒धाः शिमा॑वंतः पित्थ॒णां न शंसाः॑ सुरा॒तयः॑ ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वातासः । न । ये । धुनयः । जिगत्नवः । अग्नीनां । न । जिह्वाः । विरोकिणः ।  
> वर्मण्वंतः । न । योधाः । शिमीवंतः । पित्थाणां । न । शंसाः । सुरातयः ॥३॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ये मरुतो वातासो न वायव इव धुनयः शत्रूणां कंपयितारो
> जिगत्नवो गमनशीलाः । अत्र मारुते सूक्ते मरुतामेव दृष्टांतकथनं संचरणस्वभाववायुपदार्थतदभिमानिदेवताभेदेनाविरुद्धं । तथाग्नीनां न जिह्वा आग्नीनां
> ज्वाला इव विरोकिणो: विरोचनशीलमुखाः । तथा वर्मण्वंतो न योधाः
> कवचिनो योद्धार इव शिमीवंतः शौर्यकर्मवंतः ! तथा पितृणां न शंसाः
> पित्ृणां जनकानां शंसा वाच इव सुरातयः सुदानाः । एवंमहानुभावा एते
> मरुतोऽस्मद्यज्ञमागच्छंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Maruts who, like winds, are shakers of enemies and given to movement. Here, in a sūkta of the Maruts, the citing of the Maruts themselves as the illustration is not contradictory, through the distinction between the moving wind as a substance and the deity that presides over it. Likewise, like the tongues of fires, like flames, they have shining mouths. Likewise, like warriors in armour, they are doers of heroic deeds. Likewise, like the praises of ancestors, of fathers, they are good givers. Being of such great power, may these Maruts come to our sacrifice.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ये (*ye*)—which Maruts
- वातासो न (*vātāso na*)—like winds
- धुनयः (*dhunayaḥ*)—shakers of enemies
- जिगत्नवः (*jigatnavaḥ*)—given to movement
- अग्नीनां (*agnīnāṃ*)—of fires
- जिह्वा न (*jihvā na*)—like flames

### Printed p. 145

- विरोकिणः (*virokiṇaḥ*)—radiant
- वर्मण्वंतः (*varmaṇvaṃtaḥ*)—wearing armour
- योधाः न (*yodhāḥ na*)—like warriors
- शिमीवंतः (*śimīvaṃtaḥ*)—doers of heroic deeds
- पित्थणां (*pitthaṇāṃ*)—of ancestors
- शंसाः न (*śaṃsāḥ na*)—like praises
- सुरातयः (*surātayaḥ*)—excellent givers; may those Maruts be gracious

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May those Maruts be gracious who, like winds, shake their enemies and are given to movement, who are radiant like the flames of fires, doers of heroic deeds like warriors wearing armour, and excellent givers like the praises of ancestors.

##### English translation
(The Maruts), who like the winds cause (the foes) to tremble and move swift ; blazing like the flames of fires, (who are) the achievers of heroic deeds like warriors clad in mail ; munificent donors like the praises of ancestors

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> रथा॑नां॒ न ये॒३॒॑१राः सना॑भयो जिगी॒वांसो॒ न शूरा॑ अ॒भिद्य॑वः ।  
> व॒रे॒यवो॒ न मर्या॑ घृत॒पुषो॑ऽभिस्व॒र्तारो॑ अ॒र्कं न  ॒॑
> सुष्टुभः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> रथानां । न । ये । अराः । सनाभयः । जिगीवांसः । न । शूराः । अभिद्यवः ।  
> वरेययवः । न । मर्याः । धृतपुषः । अभिस्वर्तारः । अर्कं । न । सुस्तुभः ॥४॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> रथानां न रथचक्राणामिवाराः ते यथा बहवोऽपि सनाभयः समाननाभयो भवंति तद्विद्यॆ परस्परं सनाभयः समानबंधना एकस्मिन्नेवांतरिक्षे
> वर्तमानाः । परस्परं बंधुभूता इत्यर्थः । जिगीवांसो न शूरा जयशीलाः
> xxix 10

### Printed p. 146

> शूरा इवाभिद्यवोऽभिगतदीप्तयः किंच वरेयवो न मर्या वृतं वरं परस्मै
> प्रदातुमिच्छंतो मनुष्या इव धृतपुष उदकसेक्तारः । उदकपूर्वं हि वराणि
> वसूनि दीयंते नियमेन । वृष्टुदकप्रदा इत्यर्थः । किंचाभिस्वर्तारोर्कं
> नार्कमर्चनीयं स्तोत्रमभिस्वर्तारोऽभितः शब्दयितारो वंदिन इव सुष्टुभः
> सुशब्दाः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

As the spokes of chariot wheels, however many, have a common nave, so the Maruts have a common nave, a common bond, dwelling in the one atmosphere; that is, they are kinsmen of one another. Like victorious heroes, they are of victorious nature and have radiance turned towards all sides. Further, like generous men who wish to give a chosen boon to another, they are sprinklers of water, for chosen treasures are given always preceded by water; that is, they are givers of rain-water. Further, they are resounders on all sides of the adorable stotra, like bards, and are of excellent sound.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- रथानां (*rathānāṃ*)—of the wheels of chariots
- आराः न (*ārāḥ na*)—as the spokes
- स नाभयः (*sa nābhayaḥ*)—having a common centre
- ये (*ye*)—who
- शूराः न (*śūrāḥ na*)—like heroes
- जिगीवांसः (*jigīvāṃsaḥ*)—victorious
- अभिद्यवः (*abhidyavaḥ*)—radiant
- वरेयवः (*vareyavaḥ*)—generous
- मर्याः न (*maryāḥ na*)—like men
- धृतपुरुषः (*dhṛtapuruṣaḥ*)—pouring down rain abundantly
- अर्कं (*arkaṃ*)—the praise
- अभिस्वर्तारो न (*abhisvartāro na*)—like praisers who recite
- सुष्टुभः (*suṣṭubhaḥ*)—having excellent praise; may those Maruts be gracious

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May those Maruts be gracious who, like the spokes of chariot wheels, have one and the same place of the nave (bound by equal kinship), who are victorious and radiant like heroes, who like generous men pour down abundant rain-water, and who have excellent praise like praisers who recite the praise.

##### English translation
Who (are) united to a common centre like the spokes (of the wheels) of chariots, brilliant like victorious heroes, sprinkling water like benevolent men, sweet-voiced like men who recite praise.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अश्वा॑सो॒ न ये ज्येष्ठा॑स आ॒शवो॑ दिधि॒षवो॒ न र॒थ्यः॑ सु॒दान॑वः ।  
> आपो॒ न नि॒म्मैरु॒दभि॑र्जिग॒त्नवो॑ वि॒श्वरू॑पा॒ अंगि॑रसो॒ न  ॑
> सामभिः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अश्वासः । न । ये । ज्येष्ठासः । आशवः । दिधिषवः । न । रथ्यः । सुदानवः ।  
> आपः । न । निम्मैः । उदभिः । जिगत्नवः । विश्वरूपाः । अंगिरसः । न । सामभिः ॥५॥

### Printed p. 147

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ये मरुतोऽश्वासो नाश्वा इव ज्येष्ठाः प्रशस्यतमा आशवः शीघ्रगमनाः ।
> तथा दिधिषवो न वसूनां धारका इव रथ्यो रथस्वामिनः सुदानवः सुदानाः ।
> तथापो नाप इव निम्मैः प्रवणगैरुदभिरुदकैः सह जिगत्नवो गमनशीलाः । तथा
> विश्वरूपा नानारूपाः सामभिर्युक्ता अंगिरसो नांगिरस इव ! सर्वदा सामगा
> इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Maruts who, like horses, are the most praiseworthy and swift-going. Likewise, like holders of treasures, they are lords of chariots and good givers. Likewise, like waters with downward-flowing streams, they are given to movement. Likewise they are of many forms, accompanied by Sāmans, like the Aṅgirases; that is, they are always singers of Sāmans.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ये (*ye*)—which Maruts
- अश्वासोन (*aśvāsona*)—like horses
- ज्येष्ठासः (*jyeṣṭhāsaḥ*)—praiseworthy
- आशवः (*āśavaḥ*)—swift-going
- दिधिषवो न (*didhiṣavo na*)—like wealthy men
- रथ्यः (*rathyaḥ*)—lords of the chariot
- सुदानवः (*sudānavaḥ*)—having excellent gifts
- निम्मैः (*nimmaiḥ*)—flowing in low ground
- उदभिः (*udabhiḥ*)—with waters
- आपो न (*āpo na*)—like rivers
- जिगत्नवः (*jigatnavaḥ*)—given to movement
- विश्वरूपाः (*viśvarūpāḥ*)—having many forms
- अंगिरसो न (*aṃgiraso na*)—like the Aṅgirases
- सामभिः (*sāmabhiḥ*)—accompanied by Sāman-singing; may those Maruts be gracious

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the Maruts come graciously to our sacrifice, who are praiseworthy and swift-going like horses, lords of the chariot like wealthy men, having excellent gifts, given to movement like rivers with streams flowing in low ground, having many forms, and accompanied by Sāman-singing like the Aṅgirases.

##### English translation
Who (are) most excellent and swift like horses, lords of cars and munificent like the possessors of wealth, swiftly moving like rivers with water flowing from a height, (accompanied) everywhere with hymns like the multiform Angirasas.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ग्रावा॑णो॒ न सू॒रयः॒ सिंधु॑मातर आदर्दि॒रासो॒ अद्र॑यो॒ न  ॒॑
> विश्वहा ।  
> शि॒शूला॒ न क्री॒ळयः॑ सुमा॒तरो॑ महाग्रा॒मो न याम॑न्नु॒त  ॒
> त्तिषा ॥ ६ ॥

### Printed p. 148

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ग्रावाणः । न । सूरयः । सिंधुमातरः । आदर्दिरासः । अद्रयः । न । विश्वहा ।  
> शिशूलाः । न । क्रीळयः । सुमातरः । महाग्रामः । न । यामन् । उत । त्विषा ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सूरय उदकस्य प्रेरका ग्रावाणो न मेघा इव सिंधुमातरो नदीनिर्मातारः । आदर्दिरास आदरणशीलान्यद्रयो न वज्राद्यायुधानीव विश्वहा सर्वदा
> शत्रूणां हंतार इत्यर्थः । यद्वा । सर्वदाद्रयो न वज्रा इव शत्रूणामादर्दिरास
> आदरणशीलाः । सुमातरः शोभनमातृकाः शिशूला न शिशव इव क्रीळयो विहर्तारः ।
> उतापि च महाग्रामो न महाज्जनसंघ इव यामन्यामनि गमने त्विषा दीप्ता
> युक्ता भवंति । तेऽस्मदृज्ञमागच्छंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Like clouds, impellers of water, they are makers of rivers. Of crushing nature, like thunderbolts and other weapons, they are always destroyers of enemies. Or else: like thunderbolts, they are always crushers of enemies. Having good mothers, like children, they are sporting and wandering. And also, like a great crowd of people, in going they are endowed with splendour. May they come to our sacrifice.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सूरयः (*sūrayaḥ*)—impellers of water
- ग्रावाणो न (*grāvāṇo na*)—like clouds
- सिंधुमातरः (*siṃdhumātaraḥ*)—makers of rivers
- आदर्शिरासः (*ādarśirāsaḥ*)—cutters of enemies
- अद्रयो न (*adrayo na*)—like the thunderbolt
- विश्वहा (*viśvahā*)—destroyers of all enemies
- सुमातरः (*sumātaraḥ*)—having excellent mothers
- क्रीळयः (*krīḷayaḥ*)—fond of play
- शिशूला न (*śiśūlā na*)—like infants
- उत (*uta*)—and
- महाग्रामो न (*mahāgrāmo na*)—like a great crowd
- यामन् (*yāman*)—at the time of going
- त्विषा (*tviṣā*)—endowed with brilliance; may those Maruts be gracious

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May those Maruts be gracious who, like clouds that impel water, are makers of rivers, who like the enemy-cutting thunderbolt destroy all enemies, who have excellent mothers and are fond of play like infants, and who, like a great crowd, are endowed with brilliance at the time of going.

##### English translation
Having streams for mothers like the grinding-stones which send forth moisture; ever destroying (enemies) like destructive thunderbolts: having tender mothers like sportive infants; (endowed) with splendour like a great crowd of men in a procession.

### Printed p. 149

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒षसां॒ न के॒तवो॑ऽध्वर॒त्रियः॑ शुभं॒यवो॒ नांजिभि॒र्व्य॑श्वितन् ।  
> सिंध॑वो॒ न य॒यियो॒ भ्राज॑दृष्टयः परा॒वतो॒ न योज॑नानि ममिरे ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उषसां । न । केतवः । अध्वरश्रियः । शुभंयवः । न । अंजिभिः । वि । अश्वितन् ।  
> सिंधवः । न । ययियः । भ्राजत् ऽयष्टयः । परावतः । न । योजनानि । ममिरे॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ये मरुत उषसां न केतव उषसां रश्मय इवाध्वरश्रियो यज्ञस्या
> श्रयितारो भवंति । तथा शुभंयवो न कल्याणकामा वरा इवांजिभिराभरणैर्व्यश्वितन् दीप्यंते ॥ श्विता वर्णे । लजु रूपं ॥ तथा सिंधवो न नद्य इव ययियो
> गमनशीला भ्राजदृष्टयो दीप्यमानायुधा: परावतो न दूराध्वनीना वडवा
> इव योजनानि दूरदेशान्मिरे । परिच्छिंदंति । तेऽस्मद्यज्ञमागच्छंत्विति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Maruts who, like the rays of the dawns, become supporters of the sacrifice. Likewise, like bridegrooms desiring welfare, they shine with their ornaments. Likewise, like rivers they are given to movement and have shining weapons; like horses of far journeys they measure out leagues, that is, they cut across distant regions. May they come to our sacrifice.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उषसां (*uṣasāṃ*)—of the dawns
- केतव न (*ketava na*)—like rays
- अध्वरश्रियः (*adhvaraśriyaḥ*)—the Maruts who have the splendour of the sacrifice
- शुभंयवो न (*śubhaṃyavo na*)—like bridegrooms of auspicious nature
- अंजिभिः (*aṃjibhiḥ*)—with ornaments
- व्यश्वितन् (*vyaśvitan*)—they shine
- सिंधवो न (*siṃdhavo na*)—like rivers
- ययियः (*yayiyaḥ*)—given to movement
- भ्राजदृष्टयः (*bhrājadṛṣṭayaḥ*)—having shining weapons, the Maruts
- परावतो न (*parāvato na*)—like horses fit for long journeys
- योजनानि (*yojanāni*)—very distant regions
- ममिरे (*mamire*)—they traverse

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The Maruts, who have the splendour of the sacrifice like the rays of the dawns, shine with ornaments like bridegrooms of auspicious nature. The Maruts, who are given to movement like rivers and have shining weapons, traverse very distant regions like horses fit for long journeys.

### Printed p. 150

##### English translation
Who, radiant in sacrifices like the rays of the dawn, shine with their ornaments like those expectant of good fortune, (who) swift-moving like rivers, having bright weapons, have traversed leagues like mares who have journeyed from far, (may they come to our sacrifice).

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सु॒भा॒गान्नो॑ देवाः कृणुता सु॒रत्ना॑न॒स्मान्त्सो॒त्थन्म॑रुतो  
> नावृधा॒नाः ।  
> अधि॑ स्तो॒त्रस्य॑ स॒ख्यस्य॑ गात स॒नाद्धि वो॑ रत्न॒धेया॑नि॒ संति॑ ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सुभागान् । नः । देवाः । कृणुत । सुरत्नान् । अस्मान् । स्तोत्थन् । मरुतः ।  
> नावृधानाः ।  
> अधि । स्तोतृस्य । सख्यस्य । गात । सनात् । हि । वः । रत्नधेयानि । संति ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अनया स्तुतिमुपसंहरति । हे मरुतो देवाः ववृधानाः स्तुत्या वर्धमाना
> योयं स्तोत्थन्नोऽस्मान्सुभागान्सुधनान्कृणुत । कुरुत । तथा सुरत्नाइत्योभनरत्नान्कृणुत । यद्वा । सुरत्ना इत्योभनरमणीयस्तोत्रानस्मानिति संबंधनीयं ।
> किंच यूयं सख्यस्य स्तोत्रस्य सखिभूतं स्तोमधि गात । अधिगच्छत । वो युष्माकं
> रत्नधेयानि रत्नदानान्यस्मद्विषयाणि सनाद्भि चिरकालादारभ्य खलु संति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

With this Ṛk he concludes the praise. O Maruts, gods, you who are increased by praise, make us, the praisers, well-fortuned, that is, of good wealth. Likewise make us possessors of good jewels; or else, construe it as: us, who have good and delightful stotras. Further, you approach the praise, the stoma that has become a friend of the friendly stotra. Your gifts of jewels directed to us have indeed existed from long ago.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवाः (*devāḥ*)—divine
- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—O Maruts
- ववृधानाः (*vavṛdhānāḥ*)—you who thrive by praise
- स्तोत्कन् (*stotkan*)—praisers
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- सुभगान् (*subhagān*)—possessors of excellent wealth
- कृणुत (*kṛṇuta*)—make
- अस्मान् सुरत्नान् (*asmān suratnān*)—make us possessors of excellent jewels
- सख्यस्य (*sakhyasya*)—dear to you
- स्तोत्रस्य (*stotrasya*)—towards our praise
- अधि गात (*adhi gāta*)—come

### Printed p. 151

- वः (*vaḥ*)—in you
- रत्नधेयानि (*ratnadheyāni*)—riches that are to be given to us
- सनाद्धि संति (*sanāddhi saṃti*)—have existed for many ages

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O divine Maruts, you who thrive by praise, make us, the praisers, possessors of excellent wealth. Come towards our praise, which is dear to you. The riches that you are to give us have existed for many ages.

##### English translation
Divine Maruts, magnified (by our praise), render us, your praisers, prosperous and opulent; come this friendly laudation, for your precious bounties have been long (bestowed upon us).

## Sūkta 79 (Maṇḍala 10)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऎप्पत्तॊंभत्तनॆय सूक्तवु
> अपश्यमिति सप्तर्चमेकादशं सूक्तमाग्नेयं त्रैष्टुभं । सौचीकगुणोऽग्निऋषिर्वैश्वानरगुणो वाथवा सप्तिर्नाम वाजंभरपुत्रः । तथाचानुक्रांतं ।
> अपश्यं सप्त सौचीकोऽग्निर्वैश्वानरो वा सप्तिर्वा वाजंभर आग्नेयं त्विति ॥
> प्रातरनुवाकाश्विनशस्त्रयोस्ट्रैष्टुभे छंदसीदमादिके द्वे सूक्ते । सूत्रितं च ।
> अपश्यमस्य महत इति सूक्ते । आ. ४-१३ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning अपश्यमिति (*apaśyamiti*) has seven Ṛks; it is the eleventh sūkta, addressed to Agni, in Triṣṭubh. Its seer is Agni endowed with the quality of Saucīka, or Agni endowed with the quality of Vaiśvānara, or else Sapti by name, the son of Vājaṃbhara. So the Anukramaṇī records अपश्यं सप्त सौचीकोऽग्निर्वैश्वानरो वा सप्तिर्वा वाजंभर आग्नेयं त्विति ॥ (*apaśyaṃ sapta saucīko 'gnirvaiśvānaro vā saptirvā vājaṃbhara āgneyaṃ tviti ‖*). At the Prātaranuvāka and the Āśvinaśastra, in the Triṣṭubh metre, two sūktas beginning with this one are used. It is also formulated in the sūtra अपश्यमस्य महत इति सूक्ते (*apaśyamasya mahata iti sūkte*) (Ā. Sū. 4-13).

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta beginning अपश्यं (*apaśyaṃ*) is the eleventh sūkta in the sixth anuvāka. It has seven Ṛks. For this sūkta Agni endowed with the quality of Saucīka is the seer; or Agni endowed with the quality of Vaiśvānara is the seer; or Sapti, the son of the sage Vājaṃbhara, is the seer; Agni is the deity; Triṣṭubh is the metre. In the Anukramaṇī it is said अपश्यं सप्त सौचिकोऽग्निर्वैश्वानरो ना सप्तिर्वा वाजंभर आग्नेयं तु (*apaśyaṃ sapta sauciko 'gnirvaiśvānaro nā saptirvā vājaṃbhara āgneyaṃ tu*). That this sūkta and the next sūkta are applied for the Triṣṭubh mantras at the time of reciting the Prātaranuvāka and the Āśvinaśastra is set out by the sūtra अपश्यमस्य महत इति सूक्ते (*apaśyamasya mahata iti sūkte*) of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta-sūtra (Ā. Sū. 4-13).

### Printed p. 152

Maṇḍala 10; Anuvāka 6; Sūkta 79. Aṣṭaka 8; Adhyāya 3; Varga 14. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 7. Seer: अग्निः सौचिको वैश्वानतो ना सप्तिर्वा वाजंभरः (*agniḥ sauciko vaiśvānato nā saptirvā vājaṃbharaḥ*). Deity: अग्निः (*agniḥ*). Metre: Triṣṭubh. **NOTE**

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अप॑श्यमस्य मह॒तो म॑हि॒त्वमम॑र्त्यस्य॒ मर्त्या॑सु वि॒क्षु ।  
> नाना॒ हनू॒ विभृ॑ते॒ सं भ॑रेते॒ असि॑न्वती॒ बप्स॑ती॒ भूर्य॑त्तः ॥१॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अपश्यं । अस्य । महतः । महित्वं । अमर्त्यस्य । मर्त्यासु । विक्षु ।  
> नाना । हनू इति । विभृते इति विभृते । सं । भरेते इति । असिन्वती इति । बप्सती इति ।  
> भूरि । अत्तः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अस्याग्नेर्महतो मंहनीयस्य महित्त्वं महत्त्वमपश्यं। पश्यामि। कीदृशस्यास्य । मर्त्यासु विक्षु मनुष्यरूपासु प्रजासु तासां हृदयेऽमर्त्यस्यामरणस्वभावस्य वैश्वानररूपेण वर्तमानस्य । यद्वा। मर्त्यासु विश्वत्विग्यजमानरूपासु
> प्रजास्वमर्त्यस्य सर्वदा जागरितस्य । अथ दावाग्निरूपोऽग्निरुच्यते । अस्याग्नेर्हनू
> शिप्रे नाना विभृते विभिन्ने सं भरेते । संभरतो दंतैः । तथा कृत्वासिन्वती
> असंखादंत्यौ स्तोतारं बप्पती भक्षयंत्यौ भूरि प्रभूतं काष्ठमरण्ये वर्तमानमत्तः । भक्षयतः ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

I behold the greatness of this great, adorable Agni. Of what sort is he? He is immortal, of undying nature, existing in the form of Vaiśvānara in the hearts of the mortal peoples, the subjects in human form. Or else: immortal, ever awake in the peoples who are in the form of priests and sacrificers. Now the form of Agni as forest-fire is described: the two jaws of this Agni, parted in different ways, come together with the teeth. So doing, not chewing, devouring, they eat much wood that stands in the forest. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मर्त्यासु (*martyāsu*)—among mortal
- विक्षु (*vikṣu*)—peoples, abiding in their hearts
- अमर्त्यस्य (*amartyasya*)—deathless
- महतः (*mahataḥ*)—venerable
- अस्य (*asya*)—of this Agni
- महित्वं (*mahitvaṃ*)—the greatness
- अपश्यं (*apaśyaṃ*)—I see
- नाना (*nānā*)—in many ways
- विभृते (*vibhṛte*)—divided
- हनू (*hanū*)—the two jaws of Agni

### Printed p. 153

- संभरेते (*saṃbharete*)—are borne by the teeth
- असिन्वती (*asinvatī*)—without harming the praiser
- बप्सती (*bapsatī*)—devouring the oblation
- भूरि (*bhūri*)—abundant
- अत्तः (*attaḥ*)—they devour the wood

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

I behold the greatness of the venerable, deathless Agni, who dwells in the hearts of the mortal peoples. The two jaws of Agni, divided in many ways, are borne by the teeth. Without harming the praiser, devouring the oblation, they devour abundant wood.

##### English translation
I have beheld might of the adorable (Agni) immortal in (the hearts of) mortal beings ; his two jaws, divided asunder, shut together ; devouring without masticating consume much (fuel).

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

For the word असिन्वती (*asinvatī*), Yāska gives the explanation असिन्वती असंखादंत्यौ (*asinvatī asaṃkhādaṃtyau*) and असिन्वती बप्सती भूर्यत्तः इत्यपि निगमो भवति (*asinvatī bapsatī bhūryattaḥ ityapi nigamo bhavati*) (Ni. 6-4). That is, they devour in great quantities and in haste, without chewing well, very quickly; the idea is that they swallow quickly and simply, without chewing slowly and eating. In the next Ṛk also the words असिन्वन् (*asinvan*) and अत्ति (*atti*) are used, and the meaning there is the same.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> गुहा॒ शिरो॒ निहि॑त॒मृध॑ग॒क्षी असि॑न्वन्नत्ति जि॒ह्वया॒ वना॑नि ।  
> अत्रा॑ण्यस्मै प॒द्भिः सं भ॑रंत्युत्ता॒नह॑स्ता॒ नम॒साधि॑ वि॒क्षु ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> गुहा । शिरः । निहितं । ऋधक् । अक्षी इति । असिन्वन् । अत्ति । जिह्वया । वनानि ।  
> अत्राणि । अस्मै । पट्भिः । सं । भरंति । उत्तानहस्ताः । नमसा । अधि । विक्षु ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अस्याग्नेः शिरो मूर्धा गुहायां निहितं मनुष्योवरेषु वर्तते ।
> अस्याक्षी अक्षिणी ऋधक् पृथग्निहितं निहिते चंद्रसूर्यात्मना । तावेवाक्षिणी इत्यर्थः ॥

### Printed p. 154

> ईदृशो योऽग्निरसिन्वन्दंतैरसंखादञ्चह्वया वनान्यत्ति काष्ठानि भक्षयति दाहभूतः
> अस्मा अग्नयेऽध्वर्याद्दयः पद्भिः पादैरभिगत्यात्राणि सं भरंति । संपादयंति
> हवींषि । कथंभूता । उत्तानहस्ताः पात्रधारणार्थमुन्नतकरा नमसा नमस्कारेण
> युक्ताः । कुत्र । अधि विक्षु प्रजास्त्वत्विक्षु मध्यॆ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The head of this Agni is placed in a cave and exists among men. **NOTE** His two eyes are separately placed, in the form of the moon and the sun; these two are his eyes. This Agni, not chewing with the teeth, devours with his tongue the woods, that is, eats the timber, being the burning. To this Agni the adhvaryu and the others, going near on foot, bring oblations, that is, prepare them. How are they? With raised hands, to hold the vessels, and accompanied by obeisance. Where? Among the peoples, among the priests.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शिरः (*śiraḥ*)—the head of this Agni
- गुहा (*guhā*)—in the belly of men
- निहितं (*nihitaṃ*)—is placed
- अक्षी (*akṣī*)—the eyes of Agni
- ऋधक् (*ṛdhak*)—separate, in the form of the moon and the sun
- असिन्वन् (*asinvan*)—without chewing with the teeth
- जिह्वया (*jihvayā*)—by the tongue in the form of flame
- वनानि (*vanāni*)—the woods
- अत्ति (*atti*)—he devours
- उत्तानहस्ताः (*uttānahastāḥ*)—those with raised hands
- नमसा (*namasā*)—and accompanied by obeisances, the priests
- विक्षु अधि (*vikṣu adhi*)—among men
- अस्मै (*asmai*)—to this Agni
- पड्भिः (*paḍbhiḥ*)—going on foot
- अत्राणि (*atrāṇi*)—the oblations
- संभरंति (*saṃbharaṃti*)—they offer

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The head of this Agni is placed in the belly of men. The eyes of Agni are separate, in the form of the moon and the sun. This Agni devours the woods without chewing with the teeth, by his tongue in the form of flame alone. The priests, with raised hands and accompanied by obeisances, go on foot, approach Agni, and offer oblations to this Agni among men.

##### English translation
His head is deposited in a cavern; his eyes are vide apart; with his tongue he devours the wood without masticating ; (the priests) approaching on foot reverently offer him oblations among the people with uplifted hands.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र मा॒तुः प्र॑त॒रं गुह्य॑मि॒च्चन्कु॑मा॒रो न वी॒रुधः॑ सर्पदु॒र्वीः ।  
> स॒सं न प॒क्वम॑विदच्चु॒चंतं॑ रिरि॒ह्वांसं॑ रि॒प उ॒पस्थे॑ अं॒तः ॥ ३॥

### Printed p. 155

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । मातुः । प्रतरं । गुह्यं । इच्छन् । कुमारः । न । वीरुधः । सर्पत् । उर्वीः ।  
> ससं । न । पक्वं । अविदत् । शुचंतं । रिरिह्वांसं । रिपः । उपस्थे । अंतरिति ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अयमग्निर्मातुः पृथिव्याः संबंधिनीरुर्वीर्बह्वीर्वीरुधो लता इच्छन्
> तथा प्रतरं पृकृष्टतरं गुह्यं गोप्यं तासां वीरुधां मूलमपीच्छन्यामयमानः प्र सर्पत् । प्रसर्पति । प्रसरति । किमिव । कुमारो न कुमार इव । स यथा
> स्तन्यं पातुं जानुभ्यां सर्पति तद्वत् । ससं न पक्वं पक्वमन्नमिव शुचंतं
> दीप्यमानं नीरसं वृक्षं रिपः पृथिव्याः उपस्थेऽन्तरुत्संगेऽंतरविदत् । विंदति ।
> पुनः कीदृशं । रिरिह्वांसमाकाशमास्वादयंतं । यद्वा । मूलैर्मातरं पृथिवीं
> रिरिह्वांसं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Agni, desiring the many creepers that belong to the earth, the mother, and also the more excellent, hidden roots of those creepers, creeps forth. How? Like a child, who crawls on his knees to drink the breast. Like ripe food, he finds the shining, sapless tree in the lap of the earth, within her bosom. Of what kind is the tree? It tastes the sky. Or else: with its roots it tastes the mother, the earth.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- मातुः (*mātuḥ*)—belonging to the earth
- ऊर्विः (*ūrviḥ*)—abundant
- वीरुधः (*vīrudhaḥ*)—the creepers
- प्रतरं (*prataraṃ*)—excellently
- गुह्यं (*guhyaṃ*)—hidden within the earth, the roots of those creepers
- इच्छन् (*icchan*)—desiring
- कुमारो न (*kumāro na*)—like a young child
- प्र सर्पत् (*pra sarpat*)—he creeps
- पक्वं (*pakvaṃ*)—ripe
- ससं न (*sasaṃ na*)—like food
- शुचंतं (*śucaṃtaṃ*)—shining
- रिरिह्वांसं (*ririhvāṃsaṃ*)—embracing the atmosphere, the tree
- रिपः (*ripaḥ*)—of the earth
- उपस्थे अंतः (*upasthe aṃtaḥ*)—upon the lap
- अविदत् (*avidat*)—he obtains

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Agni, desiring the abundant creepers that belong to the earth and the roots of those creepers, excellently hidden within the earth, creeps like a young child. He obtains, upon the lap of the earth, the tree that shines like ripe food and embraces the atmosphere. That is to say, he at once burns the tree that stands dry upon the earth.

##### English translation
Longing for the abundant creepers of the maternal (earth, longing for) their more excellent concealed (root), he creeps like a child; he has found the tree shining like ripe grain, upon the lap of the earth, tasting (the ether).

### Printed p. 156

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

On the phrase ससं न पक्वं अविदत् शुचंतं (*sasaṃ na pakvaṃ avidat śucaṃtaṃ*) Yāska has given the explanation स्वपनमेतन्माध्यमिकं ज्योतिरनित्यदर्शनं तदिवादज्ञाज्वल्यमानं । (*svapanametanmādhyamikaṃ jyotiranityadarśanaṃ tadivādajñājvalyamānaṃ |*) (Ni. 5-3). Agni at once, in a gulp, eats the tree that shines, that is like ripe food, and that stands dry upon the earth. How? An object seen in a dream lasts only a moment and then becomes invisible. In the same way the lightning that appears in the atmosphere amid the clouds is seen for a moment only and at once becomes invisible. Like that, Agni eats, that is, burns, the ripe food in the form of the dry standing tree at once, that is, in the time in which one closes and opens the eye, in the shortest time. This is the meaning.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तद्वा॑मृ॒तं रो॑दसी॒ प्रब्र॑वीमि॒ जाय॑मानो मा॒तरा॒ गर्भो॑  
> अत्ति ।  
> नाहं दे॒वस्य॒ मर्त्य॑श्चिकेता॒ग्निरं॒ग विचे॑ताः॒ स प्रचे॑ताः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> • ॥ पदपातः ॥ •  
> तत् । वां । ऋतं रोदसी इति । प्र । ब्रवीमि । जायमानः । मातरा । गर्भः ।  
> अत्ति ।  
> न । अहं । देवस्य । मर्त्यः । चिकेत । अग्निः : अंग, विचेताः । सः प्रचेताः ॥४॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे रोदसी द्यावापृथिव्यौ वां युवां तदृतं सत्यं प्रब्रवीमि यदरणीभ्यां
> जायमान उत्पन्नो गर्भो गर्भस्थानीयोऽग्निर्मातरारणी अत्ति भक्षयति । देवस्य
> द्योतनादिगुणस्याग्नेर्वर्तनं मर्त्यो मनुष्योऽहं न चिकेत । न जानामि ।
> हे अंग वैश्वानर अग्निररणीभ्यामुत्पन्नो विचेता विविधज्ञानवान् स प्रचेताः
> प्रकृष्टज्ञानः । अतो न जानामीत्यर्थः । अथवांगेत्यात्मानमेव संबोध्य
> ब्रवीमि । अग्निरेव विचेता बहुधा जानाति स प्रचेताः स एव जानाति । स्वमहिमानं
> नाहं स्तोतुं प्रभवामीत्यर्थः ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O heaven and earth, I tell you this truth: Agni, born from the two fire-sticks as an embryo, as it were, devours his two mothers, the two fire-sticks. I, a mortal man, do not know the course of action of the deity Agni, whose qualities are shining and the like. O dear Vaiśvānara, Agni, born from the two fire-sticks, is of manifold knowledge; he is of excellent knowledge. Therefore I do not know, such is the meaning. **NOTE** Or else, addressing himself as "dear one", he says: Agni alone knows in manifold ways; he, of excellent knowledge, alone knows. I am not able to praise his own greatness; such is the meaning.

### Printed p. 157

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- रोदसी (*rodasī*)—O heaven and earth
- नां (*nāṃ*)—to you
- तत् ऋतं (*tat ṛtaṃ*)—this truth
- प्र ब्रवीमि (*pra bravīmi*)—I declare
- जायमानः (*jāyamānaḥ*)—as soon as he is born
- गर्भः (*garbhaḥ*)—Agni in the form of an embryo
- मातरा (*mātarā*)—his mothers, the two fire-sticks
- अत्ति (*atti*)—devours
- देवस्य (*devasya*)—of Agni, whose nature is divine, the nature
- मर्त्यः (*martyaḥ*)—a man
- अहं (*ahaṃ*)—I
- न चिकेत (*na ciketa*)—cannot know
- आंग (*āṃga*)—O sacrificer
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni, born from the fire-stick
- विचेताः (*vicetāḥ*)—possessed of manifold knowledge
- स प्रचेताः (*sa pracetāḥ*)—possessed of most excellent knowledge

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O heaven and earth, I declare to you this truth. As soon as he is born, Agni in the form of an embryo devours his mothers, the two fire-sticks (the trees). I, a man, cannot know the nature of Agni, whose nature is divine. O sacrificer, Agni, born from the fire-stick, is possessed of manifold knowledge and of most excellent knowledge.

##### English translation
This (that) I declare unto you, heaven and earth, (is) the truth; as soon as born, the embryo devours the parents ; I who am a mortal know not (the condition) of the deity ; Agni, (O Vaishwanara), is discriminating he is exceedingly wise.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यो अ॑स्मा॒ अन्नं॑ तृ॒ष्णा॒३॒॑ऽदधा॒त्याज्यै॑र्घृ॒तैर्जु॒होति॒ पुष्य॑ति ।  
> तस्यै॑ स॒हस्र॑म॒क्षभि॒र्वि च॒क्षेऽग्ने॑ वि॒श्वतः॑ प्र॒त्यज्ज॑सि॒ त्वं ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । अस्मै । अन्नं । तृषु । आदधाति । आज्यैः । घृतैः । जुहोति । पुष्यति ।  
> तस्मै । सहस्रं । अक्षभिः । वि । चक्षे । अग्ने । विश्वतः । प्रत्यज् । असि । त्वं ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यो यजमानोऽस्मा अग्नये तृषु क्षिप्रमन्नमादधाति करोत्याज्यैर्घृतैः
> क्षरद्रूपैर्दीप्तैर्वा सोमरसैर्जुहोति पुष्यति पुष्णाति चैनं काष्ठैः तस्यॆ ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sacrificer who quickly provides this Agni with food, makes oblations to him with ghee, which is flowing, or with shining Soma-juices, and nourishes him with logs of wood: him, the sacrificer, is the meaning.

### Printed p. 158

> तमित्यर्थः । तं सहस्रं सहस्रसंख्याकैरक्षभिरक्षिस्थानीयाभिरपरिमिताभिर्ज्वालाभिर्वि
> चक्षे । विचष्टे । विपश्यति । अथ प्रत्यक्षकृतः । हे अग्ने त्वं विश्वतः सर्वतः प्रत्यज्जसि ।
> प्रत्यगंचनो भवसि । अस्मदानुकूल्येन प्रवर्तमानो भवसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Him Agni looks upon with a thousand eyes, that is, with countless flames that stand in the place of eyes; he sees him with favour. Now, turned to him directly: O Agni, you are on every side turned towards us; you act in a manner favourable to us.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—the sacrificer who
- अस्मै (*asmai*)—for this Agni
- तृषु (*tṛṣu*)—quickly
- अन्नं (*annaṃ*)—food
- आ दधाति (*ā dadhāti*)—offers
- आज्यैः धृतैः (*ājyaiḥ dhṛtaiḥ*)—with flowing ghee, or with flowing Soma-juices
- जुहोति (*juhoti*)—offers the oblation
- पुष्यति (*puṣyati*)—nourishes by offering logs of wood and the like
- तस्मै (*tasmai*)—that sacrificer
- सहस्रं (*sahasraṃ*)—a thousand in number
- अक्षभिः (*akṣabhiḥ*)—with eyes
- वि चक्षे (*vi cakṣe*)—Agni looks upon
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- विश्वतः (*viśvataḥ*)—in every respect
- प्रत्यज् असि (*pratyaj asi*)—you are favourable to us

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The sacrificer who quickly offers food to this Agni, and offers the oblation in the form of flowing Soma-juice, and nourishes him with logs of wood and the like, that sacrificer Agni looks upon with a thousand eyes (with a mind of favour). O Agni, you are favourable to us in all matters.

##### English translation
He who quickly offers him food, makes oblations to him with dripping Soma-juices, and delights him (with food); (Agni) looks upon him with a thousand eyes; you Agni, are everywhere present.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> किं दे॒वेषु॒ त्यज॒ एन॑श्चक॒र्थाग्ने॑ पृ॒च्छामि॒ नु त्वामवि॑द्वान् ।  
> अक्री॑ळ॒न् क्रीळ॒न्नरि॒रत्त॑वे॒दन्नि प॑र्व॒शश्च॑कर्त॒ गामि॑वा॒सिः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> किं । देवेषु । त्यजः । एनः । चकर्थ । अग्ने । पृच्छामि । नु । त्वां । अविद्वान् ।  
> अक्रीळन् । क्रीळन् । हरिः । अत्तवे । अदन् । वि । पर्वशः । चकर्थ । गां इव । असिः ॥

### Printed p. 159

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने त्वां नु क्षिप्रं देवेषु किं त्यजः क्रोधमेन? पापं च चकर्थ
> कृतवानसीत्यविद्वान्हं पृच्छामि । खांडवं दहंतमग्निं पृच्छामि । किंचाक्रीळन्
> क्वचिद्देशेऽविहरन् क्रीळन् क्वचिद्देशे विनोदयल्लीलया दहन्हरिर्हरितवर्णः
> सन्नत्तवेऽत्तव्यमदन्नकाष्ठादिकं पर्वशः संधौ संधौ वि चकर्थ । विविधं करोषि ।
> गामिवासिः गां यथासिः स्वधितिः पर्वशश्चि नत्ति तद्वत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, I ask you quickly: what wrath and what sin have you committed among the gods? I ask in ignorance. I ask Agni, who burned the Khāṇḍava forest. Further: not sporting in one place, sporting in another, burning playfully, being of green hue, eating what is to be eaten, such as wood and the like, you divide it variously, joint by joint. Like a knife, which cuts a cow joint by joint, so you do.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- त्वां (*tvāṃ*)—you
- नु (*nu*)—quickly
- देवेषु (*deveṣu*)—among the gods
- किं त्यजः (*kiṃ tyajaḥ*)—what wrathful act
- एनः (*enaḥ*)—sin
- चकर्थ (*cakartha*)—you have committed
- अविद्वान् (*avidvān*)—I, who do not know
- पृच्छामि (*pṛcchāmi*)—ask
- आक्रीळन् (*ākrīḷan*)—not sporting in some places
- क्रीळन् (*krīḷan*)—sporting in some places
- हरिः (*hariḥ*)—Agni, being of green hue
- आत्तवे (*āttave*)—what is to be eaten
- अदन् (*adan*)—eating
- गां (*gāṃ*)—a cow
- आसिः (*āsiḥ*)—as a knife cuts
- पर्वशः (*parvaśaḥ*)—in the joints
- वि चकर्थ (*vi cakartha*)—he splits

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, I who do not know ask you what wrathful act and what sin you have committed among the gods. Being of green hue, Agni, sporting in some places and not sporting in others, eating what is to be eaten, splits it in the joints, as a knife cuts up an animal (for its flesh).

##### English translation
What wrathful act, what sin have you committed among the gods? Ignorant I ask you, Agni, sporting (here) not sporting (there), golden-hued ; eating what is to be eaten, you cut (your food) into pieces as the knife (cuts up) the cow.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> विसू॑चो॒ अश्वा॑न्युयुजे वने॒जा ऋजी॑तिभी रश॒नाभि॑र्गृभी॒तान् ।  
> च॒क्ष॒दे मि॒त्रो वसु॑भिः॒ सुजा॑तः॒ समा॑नृधे॒ पर्व॑भिर्वा वृधा॒नः॥

### Printed p. 160

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ॥ पपसाधे ॥  
> विषूचः । अश्वान् । युयुजे । वनेऽजाः । ऋजीतिभिः । रशनाभिः । गृभीतान् ।  
> चक्षदे । मित्रः । वसुऽभिः । सुऽजातः । सं । आन्यधे । पर्वऽभिः । ववृधानः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अयमग्निर्विषूचो विश्वगंचनानश्वान्वाप्तान्महतो वृक्षान्सर्वतो
> गंत्यनश्वानेव वा युयुजे युक्ते वनेजा वने प्रवृद्धः संपन्नः सन् ।
> कीदृशानश्वान् । ऋजीतिभिर्यजुगामिनीभी रशनाभी रशनास्थानीयाभिर्लताभिर्गृभीतान्परिगृहीतान्वेषितान् । साक्षादश्वपक्ष ऋजुगतिभिः प्रसिद्धाभी रशनाभिर्गृभीतान्गृहीतान्स्कीयानश्वाइच्छॆ दाहाय रथे योजितवानित्यर्थः । तादृशोऽग्निश्चक्षदे
> चक्षाद शकलीकरोति वसूनि मित्रोऽस्माकं मित्रभूतो वसुभिर्वासकै रश्मिभिः
> सुजातः सुष्टु प्रवृद्धः सन् । किंच समानृधे सम्यग्वर्धते पर्वभिः काष्ठखंडैर्वा.
> वृधानो वर्धमानः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Agni, born and grown in the forest, has yoked the horses that go in every direction, that is, the great trees that have spread out, going everywhere, or else the horses themselves. Of what kind are the horses? They are held by reins that go straight, that is, by creepers that stand in the place of reins. **NOTE** Such an Agni "breaks into pieces" the goods; **NOTE** he is a friend, our friend, and is well born, that is, well grown, by the rays that cause dwelling. Further, he thrives fully, growing by joints, that is, by pieces of wood.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वनेजा (*vanejā*)—Agni, born in the forest
- ऋजीतिभि (*ṛjītibhi*)—that carry along a straight path
- रशनाभि (*raśanābhi*)—with reins
- गृभीतान् (*gṛbhītān*)—held
- विषूचः (*viṣūcaḥ*)—going everywhere
- अश्वान् (*aśvān*)—the horses
- युयुजे (*yuyuje*)—he yokes
- मित्रः (*mitraḥ*)—dear to us
- वसुभि (*vasubhi*)—with riches
- सुजातः (*sujātaḥ*)—nourished, Agni
- चक्षदे (*cakṣade*)—he provides us with riches
- पर्वभि (*parvabhi*)—with pieces of wood
- वावृधानः (*vāvṛdhānaḥ*)—having grown
- समानृधे (*samānṛdhe*)—he becomes strong

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Agni, born in the forest, yokes the horses that go everywhere, held by reins that carry along a straight path. Agni, who is dear to us and strong with riches, provides us with riches. Growing by pieces of wood, he becomes strong.

##### English translation
Born in the woods, he has harnessed his steeds which move everywhere held in by straight-guiding reins ; friendly and augmented by rays, he has distributed (wealth); he grows in strength nourished with logs of wood.

## Sūkta 80 (Maṇḍala 10)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अग्निः सप्तिमिति सप्तर्चं द्वादशं सूक्तं सौचीकस्य वैश्वानरस्याग्नेरार्षं
> त्रैष्टुभमाग्नेयं ॥ अग्निः सप्तिमित्यनुक्रांतं ॥ प्रातरनुवाकाश्विनशस्त्रयोरुक्तो
> विनियोगः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning अग्निः सप्तिं (*agniḥ saptiṃ*) has seven Ṛks and is the twelfth sūkta. Its seer is Agni Saucīka Vaiśvānara; its deity is Agni; its metre is Triṣṭubh. The Anukramaṇī records it with the words अग्निः सप्तिमित्यनुक्रांतं ॥ (*agniḥ saptimityanukrāṃtaṃ ‖*) Its use at the Prātaranuvāka and at the Āśvinaśastra has been stated before.

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta beginning अग्निः सप्तिं (*agniḥ saptiṃ*) is the twelfth sūkta in the sixth anuvāka. It has seven Ṛks. As for the preceding sūkta, the seer of this sūkta is Agni Saucīka, or Agni Vaiśvānara, or Sapti, the son of Vājaṃbhara; Agni is the deity; Triṣṭubh is the metre. In the Anukramaṇī it is said अग्निः सप्तिं (*agniḥ saptiṃ*). It has been stated before that this sūkta is used for the Prātaranuvāka and for the mantras of the Āśvinaśastra.

Maṇḍala 10; Anuvāka 6; Sūkta 80. Aṣṭaka 8; Adhyāya 3; Varga 15. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 7. Seer: अग्निः सौचिको वैश्वानरो वा (*agniḥ sauciko vaiśvānaro vā*). **NOTE** Deity: अग्निः (*agniḥ*). Metre: Triṣṭubh.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निः सप्तिं॑ वाजंभ॒रं द॑दात्य॒ग्निर्वी॒रं श्रुत्यं॑ कर्मनि॒ष्ठां ।  
> अ॒ग्नी रोद॑सी॒ वि च॑रत्समं॒जन्न॒ग्निर्नारीं॑ वी॒रकु॑क्षिं॒ पुरं॑धिं ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निः । सप्तिं । वाजंभरं । ददाति. अग्निः । वीरं । श्रुत्यं । कर्मनिःस्थां ।  
> अग्निः । रोदसी इति । वि । चरत् । सं अंजन् । अग्निः । नारीं । वीरकुक्षिं पुरंधिं॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अयमग्निः सप्तिं सर्पणस्वभावमश्वं वाजंभरं युद्धे शत्रूञ्जित्वान्न
> संपादकं ददाति स्तोतृभ्यः । तथाग्निर्वीरं वीर्यवंतं पुत्रं श्रुत्यं पितृव्यपदेशकं
> xxix 11
> xxix 11

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Agni gives to the singers a horse (सप्तिं (*saptiṃ*)), of a creeping nature, one that brings food in battle by conquering the enemies (वाजंभरं (*vājaṃbharaṃ*)). Likewise Agni gives a hero, a son full of valour, renowned (श्रुत्यं (*śrutyaṃ*)), one who makes his father known,

### Printed p. 162

> कर्मनिःष्ठां यागेऽनन्यचित्तं ददाति । अग्नी रोदसी द्यावापृथिव्यौ समंजन्
> सम्यगंजयन्वि चरत् । विविधं चरति । अयमग्निर्नारीं योषितं वीरकुक्षिं
> वीरप्रसवकुक्षिं पुरंथिं च करोति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

a son intent on ritual, with an undistracted mind in the sacrifice; he gives. Agni, travelling variously (वि चरत् (*vi carat*)), makes the two worlds, heaven and earth, shine well (समंजन् (*samaṃjan*)). **NOTE** This Agni makes a woman have a womb that bears heroes, and makes her endowed with understanding (पुरंधिं (*puraṃdhiṃ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- वाजंभरं (*vājaṃbharaṃ*)—that brings food in battle
- सप्तिं (*saptiṃ*)—a horse
- ददाति (*dadāti*)—gives to the sacrificer
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- वीरं (*vīraṃ*)—valiant
- श्रुत्यं (*śrutyaṃ*)—renowned
- कर्मनिष्ठां (*karmaniṣṭhāṃ*)—intensely devoted to ritual; he gives such a son
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- रोदसी (*rodasī*)—the two, heaven and earth
- समंजन् (*samaṃjan*)—pervading
- विचरत् (*vicarat*)—he travels in many ways
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- नारीं (*nārīṃ*)—a noble woman
- वीरकुक्षिं (*vīrakukṣiṃ*)—so that she bears a hero as son
- पुरंधिं (*puraṃdhiṃ*)—and endowed with understanding, he makes her

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Agni gives to the sacrificer a horse that brings food in battle. Agni gives a son who is valiant, renowned and intensely devoted to ritual. Agni pervades heaven and earth and travels in many ways. Agni makes a noble woman bear a hero as son and makes her endowed with understanding.

##### English translation
Agni gives (his worshipper) a food-acquiring steed, Agni gives (him) a valiant son, renowned,lassiduous in pious works ; Agni travels beautifying heaven and earth; Agni (makes) a woman the bearer of male off-spring (and) intelligent. **NOTE**

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्नेरप्प॑सः स॒मिद॑स्तु भ॒द्राग्निर्म॒ही रोद॑सी॒ आ वि॑वेश ।  
> अ॒ग्निरेकं॑ चोदयत्स॒मत्स्त्व॒ग्निर्वृ॒त्राणि॑ दयते पु॒रूणि॑ ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्नेः । अप्पसः । संइत् । अस्तु । भद्रा । अग्निः । मही इति । रोदसी इति । आ ।  
> विवेश ।  
> अग्निः । एकं । चोदयत् । समत्सु । अग्निः । वृत्राणि । दयते । पुरूणि ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 163

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अप्नसः कर्मवतोऽग्नेः समिधद्रास्तु । अयमग्निर्मही महत्यौ रोदसी
> द्यावापृथिव्याना विवेश । आविष्टवान्स्वतेजसा । तथायमग्निरेकमसहायमेव
> चोदयत् चोदयति प्रेरयति समत्तु संग्रामेषु । युद्धे स्वभक्तं स्वयं
> सहायः सञ्वयिनं करोतीत्यर्थः । तथाग्निर्वृत्राणि पुरूणि बहूञ्यत्रून्दयते । हंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

May the fuel-stick of Agni, the doer of rites (अप्नसः (*apnasaḥ*)), be auspicious. This Agni has entered the two great worlds, heaven and earth, with his own brilliance. Likewise this Agni urges on the one, the unassisted, in battles: he makes his own devotee victorious in war, himself becoming his helper; such is the meaning. Likewise Agni slays the many enemies.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अप्नसः (*apnasaḥ*)—of the doer of rites
- अग्नेः (*agneḥ*)—of Agni
- समित् (*samit*)—the fuel-stick
- भद्रा (*bhadrā*)—auspicious
- अस्तु (*astu*)—may it be
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- मही (*mahī*)—the great
- रोदसी (*rodasī*)—heaven and earth
- आ विवेश (*ā viveśa*)—he has entered
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- एकं (*ekaṃ*)—as one without a second **NOTE**
- चोदयत् (*codayat*)—he urges on
- समत्सु (*samatsu*)—in battles
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- पुरूणि (*purūṇi*)—very many
- वृत्राणि (*vṛtrāṇi*)—enemies
- दयते (*dayate*)—he slays

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the fuel-stick of Agni, the doer of rites, be auspicious. Agni has entered the great heaven and earth. Agni, without a second, urges on the sacrificer; in battles Agni slays very many enemies.

##### English translation
May the fuel of the efficient Agni be auspicious; Agni has penetrated the vast heaven and earth; Agni animates (the worshipper) alone in battles; Agni destroys numerous enemies.

> अग्निः । अत्रिं । घर्मे । उरुष्यत् । अंतः । अग्निः । नृमेधं। प्रजया । असृजत्  
> सं ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अग्निर्हायमग्निः खलु त्यं तं प्रसिद्धं जरतः कर्णं जरत्कर्णनामानमृषिमाव । ररक्ष । तथायमग्निरद्भो जरूधमेतन्नामानमसुरं निरदहत् । निर्दग्धवान् । तथाग्निरत्रिं महर्षीं घर्मेंतर्ऋबीसेवस्थितमुरुष्यत् । ररक्ष ।
> तथायमग्निर्नृमेधमेतन्नामकमृषिं प्रजया पुत्रादिलक्षणया समसृजत् ।
> संयुयोज ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Agni, indeed, protected that well-known sage named Jaratkarṇa. Likewise this Agni burnt out the asura named Jarūtha. **NOTE** Likewise Agni protected the great sage Atri, who was staying in the hot pit; **NOTE** Likewise this Agni joined the sage named Nṛmedha with progeny, which consists of sons and the like.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- त्यं (*tyaṃ*)—the well-known
- जरतः कर्णं (*jarataḥ karṇaṃ*)—the sage called Jaratkarṇa
- आव (*āva*)—has protected
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- अद्भ्यः (*adbhyaḥ*)—with flames
- जरूथं (*jarūthaṃ*)—the asura called Jarūtha
- निरदहत् (*niradahat*)—has burnt
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- अत्रिं (*atriṃ*)—the great sage Atri
- घर्मे अंतः (*gharme aṃtaḥ*)—from the burning pit
- उरुष्यत् (*uruṣyat*)—he protected
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- नृमेधं (*nṛmedhaṃ*)—the sage called Nṛmedha
- प्रजया (*prajayā*)—with offspring
- समसृजत् (*samasṛjat*)—he joined

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Agni has protected the well-known sage called Jaratkarṇa. Agni has burnt with his flames the asura called Jarūtha. He has protected the great sage Atri from the burning fire-pit. He has caused the sage called Nṛmedha to obtain offspring.

##### English translation
Agni verily has protected Jaratkarna; he has consumed Jarutha with his flames; Agni rescued Atri in the hot fissure; Agni furnished Nrimedha with progeny.

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अयमग्निर्वीरपेशाः प्रेरकज्वालारूपो द्रविणं धनं दात् । ददाति ।
> तथाग्निर्ऋषिं मंत्रद्रष्टारं पुत्रं प्रयच्छतीति शेषः । ऋषिर्विशेष्यते । य ऋषिः
> सहस्रा गवां सहस्राणि सनोति भजते दक्षिणात्वेन तम्मृषिं ददातीति । तथाग्निर्दिवि
> द्युलोके हव्यं यजमानैर्हुतमा ततान । देवेषु विस्तारयति । तथाग्नेर्धामानि
> शरीराणि विभृता विभृतानि पुरुत्रा बहुषु स्थानेष्वाहवनीयधिष्णादिषु
> क्षित्यादिसु ना ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Agni, whose form is impelling flames (वीरपेशाः (*vīrapeśāḥ*)), gives wealth. Likewise Agni gives a seer, a seer of mantras, as a son; "he gives" is to be supplied. The seer is qualified: the seer who obtains a thousand cows as sacrificial fee, that seer he gives. **NOTE** Likewise Agni has spread the oblation offered by the sacrificers in the heavenly world; he extends it among the gods. Likewise the bodies of Agni are distributed in many places, in the Āhavanīya, the dhiṣṇya hearths and the like, on earth and elsewhere. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- वीरपेशाः (*vīrapeśāḥ*)—with spreading flames
- द्रविणं (*draviṇaṃ*)—wealth
- दात् (*dāt*)—gives
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which seer
- सहस्र (*sahasra*)—a thousand in number, cows
- सनोति (*sanoti*)—is fit to obtain
- ऋषिं (*ṛṣiṃ*)—to such a seer
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni gives sons and the like
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- दिवि (*divi*)—in the heavenly world
- हव्यं (*havyaṃ*)—the oblation
- आ ततान (*ā tatāna*)—has made to spread
- अग्नेः (*agneḥ*)—of Agni
- धामानि (*dhāmāni*)—the bodies
- पुरुत्रा (*purutrā*)—in many places
- विभृता (*vibhṛtā*)—become manifest

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Agni, with spreading flames, gives wealth. To the seer who is fit to obtain a thousand cows Agni gives sons and the like. Agni has made the oblation spread in the heavenly world. The bodies of Agni become manifest in many places.

##### English translation
Agni sending forth flames bestows wealth ; Agni (gives a son to) the Rishi who acquires a thousand cows; Agni spreads the oblation through heaven, the forms of Agni are manifested in many places.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निर्मु॒र्क्षैर्ऋष॑यो॒ वि ह्व॑यंते॒ऽग्निं नरो॒ याम॑नि  
> बाधि॒तासः॑ ।  
> अ॒ग्निं वयो॑ अं॒तरि॑क्षे॒ पतं॑तो॒ऽग्निः स॒हस्रा॒ परि॑ याति॒ गोनां॑॥

### Printed p. 166

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निं । उक्षैः । ऋषयः । वि । ह्वयंते । अग्निं । नरः । यामनि । बाधितासः ।  
> अग्निं । वयः । अंतरिक्षे । पतंतः । अग्निः । सहस्रा । परि । याति । गोनां ॥ ५ ५

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अग्निमुक्थैः शस्त्रैर्ऋषयः पूर्वे वि ह्वयंते । विविधमाह्वयंति यज्ञे
> स्वीये । अग्निं नरो मनुष्या यामनि संग्रामे । यातिर्वधकर्मसु पठितः ।
> बाधितासः शत्रुभ्यो बाधिताः प्राप्नुवंति जयार्थं । तथाग्निं वयः पक्षिणोऽतरिक्षे
> पतंतः पश्यंति रात्रिषु । तस्मादिमां वयांसि नक्तं नाध्यासते । तै. सं. ५-६-४-४ ।
> इति ब्राह्मणं । यद्वा । दावभूतमग्निमंतरिक्षगा वयः पतंति । तथाग्निर्गोनां
> सहस्रा सहस्राणि परि परितो याति । गच्छति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The ancient seers invoke Agni variously with uktha-hymns, the śastras, at their own sacrifice. Men invoke Agni in battle (यामनि (*yāmani*)); the word याति (*yāti*) is read among the words for killing. Those who are hard pressed by their enemies approach him for victory. Likewise the birds flying in the atmosphere see Agni in the nights; तस्मादिमां वयांसि नक्तं नाध्यासते । (*tasmādimāṃ vayāṃsi naktaṃ nādhyāsate |*) (Tai. Saṃ. 5-6-4-4) इति ब्राह्मणं (*iti brāhmaṇaṃ*) **GLOSS** Or else: birds flying in the atmosphere fly towards Agni, who has become a forest-fire. Likewise Agni goes all around the thousands of cattle.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऋषयः (*ṛṣayaḥ*)—the seers
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- उक्षैः (*ukṣaiḥ*)—with śastra-mantras
- वि ह्वयंते (*vi hvayaṃte*)—they call in many ways
- यामनि (*yāmani*)—in battle
- बाधितासः (*bādhitāsaḥ*)—when greatly harmed
- नरः (*naraḥ*)—men
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—they call Agni
- अंतरिक्षे (*aṃtarikṣe*)—in the sky
- पतंतः (*pataṃtaḥ*)—flying
- वयः (*vayaḥ*)—the birds
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—call Agni
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- गोनां सहस्रा (*gonāṃ sahasrā*)—cows in great number
- परि याति (*pari yāti*)—surrounds

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The seers call Agni in many ways with śastra-mantras. When men are greatly harmed in battle, they call Agni. The birds flying in the sky call Agni. Agni surrounds cows in great number.

##### English translation
The Rishis variously invoke Agni with hymns; men when hard pressed in battle (invoke) Agni; the birds flying in mid-heaven (invoke) Agni; Agni circumambulates thousands of cattle.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निं विश॑ ईळते॒ मानु॑षी॒र्या अ॒ग्निं मानु॑षो॒ नहु॑षो॒ वि  ॒
> जाताः ।  
> अ॒ग्निर्गांध॑र्वीं प॒थ्या॑मृ॒तस्या॒ग्नेर्गव्यू॑तिर्घृ॒त आ निष॑त्ता ॥६॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निं । विशः । ईळते । मानुषीः । याः । अग्निं । मनुषः । नहुषः । वि । जाताः ।  
> अग्निं । गांधर्विं । पथ्यां । ऋतस्य । अग्नेः । गव्योतिः । धृते । आ । निसत्ता ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अग्निं विशः प्रजा ऋत्विग्यजमानलक्षणा ईळते । स्तुवंति । या मानुषीर्मानुष्यो मनुष्याज्जाताः ता ईळते । तथाग्निं मनुषो मनुष्या नहुषो
> राज्ञः सकाशाज्जाताः प्रजा विविधं स्तुवंतीति । अग्निर्गांधर्वीं । वाज्नामैतत् ।
> वाचु शृणोति । कीदृशीं गांधर्वीं ! ऋतस्य यज्ञस्य पथ्यां पथि हितां ।
> अग्नेर्महात्मनो गव्यूतिर्मार्गो धृत आज्य आ निषत्ता सर्वतो निषण्णो भवति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The subjects (विशः (*viśaḥ*)), the people made up of priests and sacrificers, praise Agni; those who are human, born of men, praise him. Likewise men, the subjects born of a king, of Nahuṣa, variously praise Agni. Agni hears the Gāndharvī: this is a name of speech. Of what kind? That which is favourable on the path of the ṛta, the sacrifice. The path of the great Agni is established, seated on all sides, in the ghee.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- याः (*yāḥ*)—who
- मानुषीः (*mānuṣīḥ*)—are human
- विशः (*viśaḥ*)—such subjects
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- ईळते (*īḷate*)—they praise
- नहुषः राज्ञः (*nahuṣaḥ rājñaḥ*)—by king Nahuṣa
- जाताः (*jātāḥ*)—born, the subjects also
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—they praise Agni
- ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)—for the sacrifice
- पथ्यां (*pathyāṃ*)—beneficial
- गांधर्वीं (*gāṃdharvīṃ*)—the speech of praise
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni hears
- अग्नेः (*agneḥ*)—of Agni
- गव्यूतिः (*gavyūtiḥ*)—the path
- धृते (*dhṛte*)—in ghee
- आ निषत्वा (*ā niṣatvā*)—is established

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

All the human subjects praise Agni. All the subjects born of Nahuṣa praise Agni. Agni hears the speech of praise that is beneficial to the sacrifice. The path of Agni is established in the ghee.

### Printed p. 168

##### English translation
The races who are of human birth praise Agni, so do the men descended from king Nahush ; Agni, (hears) the voice which is fit for the path of sacrifice ; Agni's path lies everywhere in ghi.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्नये॒ ब्रह्म॑ ऋ॒भव॑स्ततक्षुर॒ग्निं म॒हाम॑वोचामा सुवृ॒क्तिं।  
> अग्ने॒ प्राव॑ जरि॒तारं॑ यवि॒ष्ठाग्ने॒ महि॒ द्रवि॑ण॒मा य॑जस्व ॥७॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्नये । ब्रह्म । ऋभवः । ततक्षुः । अग्निं । महां । अवोचाम । सुवृक्तिं ।  
> अग्ने । प्र । अव । जरितारं । यविष्ठ । अग्ने । महि । द्रविणं । आ । यजस्व ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अग्नये ब्रह्म स्तोत्रमृभवो मेधाविनस्ततक्षुः । अकुर्वन् । वयं च
> महां महांतमग्निं प्रति सुवृक्तिं स्तुतिमवोचाम । हे यविष्ठ युवतमाग्ने
> जरितारं स्तोतारं प्राव । प्ररक्ष । हे अग्ने महि महद्द्रविणं धनमा यजस्व ।
> प्रयच्छेत्यर्थः । अत्र प्रतिवाक्यमग्न्यभिधानं तस्य स्तुत्यत्वप्रदर्शनार्थं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Ṛbhus, the wise, fashioned a hymn of praise (ब्रह्म (*brahma*)) for Agni; and we have recited a good praise to the great Agni. O youngest Agni, protect the singer, the praiser. O Agni, grant great wealth. Here the name of Agni is repeated in every sentence in order to show that he is the one to be praised.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऋभवः (*ṛbhavaḥ*)—the wise Ṛbhus
- अग्नये (*agnaye*)—addressed to Agni
- ब्रह्म (*brahma*)—a hymn of praise
- ततक्षुः (*tatakṣuḥ*)—have fashioned
- महां (*mahāṃ*)—great
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—addressed to Agni
- सुवृक्तिं (*suvṛktiṃ*)—an excellent praise
- आवोचाम (*āvocāma*)—we have recited
- यविष्ठ (*yaviṣṭha*)—exceedingly young
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- जरितारं (*jaritāraṃ*)—your praiser
- प्राव (*prāva*)—protect
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- महि (*mahi*)—great
- द्रविणं (*draviṇaṃ*)—wealth
- आ यजस्व (*ā yajasva*)—provide

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The wise Ṛbhus have fashioned a hymn of praise addressed to Agni. We have recited an excellent praise addressed to the great Agni. O Agni, exceedingly young, protect your praiser. O Agni, provide great wealth.

### Printed p. 169

##### English translation
The Ribhus have fabricated praise for Agni; we have recited pious praise to the mighty Agni; Agni, youngest (of the gods), protect your worshipper; Agni, bestow (on him) abundant wealth.

## Sūkta 81 (Maṇḍala 10)

**Introduction**

Sūktas 81 and 82 of the tenth maṇḍala, composed by the seer Viśvakarman, contain praise of the deity named Viśvakarman. Sāyaṇa and others have explained the mantras of these sūktas with reference to the sacrifice and also with reference to the self. The sūktas composed by seers such as Aghamarṣaṇa, Prajāpati, Parameṣṭhin, Brahmaṇaspati, Dīrghatamas and Hiraṇyagarbha all give an account of the creation, the maintenance and the rest of the world. But these sūktas do not all convey one and the same view. Concerning the maker of the world, or the nature of his resolve, or the primal cause of the world, not all the seers hold one view. In each sūkta there is a different kind of account; only in some of the main matters are they alike. The account तमिद्दर्भं प्रथमं दध्र अपः (*tamiddarbhaṃ prathamaṃ dadhra apaḥ*) (Śu. Saṃ. 10-82-6) **GLOSS** **NOTE** makes known the view of the seer Viśvakarman. The account of creation and of its order will be given in the Special Topics of the respective Ṛks. First, what the nature of Viśvakarman is, what the relation is between him and the created world, and such matters have to be examined here.

विश्वकर्मा सर्वस्य कर्ता (*viśvakarmā sarvasya kartā*) (Ni. 10-25). The etymologists of the Nirukta explain that he is named Viśvakarman because he makes and perfects everything, that is, because he has created everything. Durgācārya, explaining this, says: यदिदं किंचिद्भूतं भविष्यमाणं, क्रियमाणं च तस्य सर्वस्य कर्ता । (*yadidaṃ kiṃcidbhūtaṃ bhaviṣyamāṇaṃ, kriyamāṇaṃ ca tasya sarvasya kartā |*) (Ni. Bhā. 10-25) **NOTE** That is to say, he has this name because he produces and

### Printed p. 170

refines all that existed before, all that will come to be, and all that is yet to be made. The Bṛhaddevatā also says this and explains the derivation of the word:

निदाघमासातिगमे यदृतेनावति क्षितिं । विश्वस्य जनयन्कर्म विश्वकर्मृष तेन सः ॥ (*nidāghamāsātigame yadṛtenāvati kṣitiṃ | viśvasya janayankarma viśvakarmṛṣa tena saḥ ‖*) (Bṛ. De. 2-50)

After the hot season (nidāgha) has passed, he makes the earth fertile with water and causes every kind of work to be begun in the world. Therefore the name is Viśvakarman. Viśvakarman also has the epithet Bhauvana. Some take it to mean the son of Bhuvana. If the spiritual sense is examined, this meaning does not result.

विश्वकर्मा भुवनानां भूतानामात्मनि होता भूतेषु चात्मानं ; तथा हि विज्ञायते ; हुताहं भूतेश्वात्मानं जुहवानि भूतानि चात्मनि । (*viśvakarmā bhuvanānāṃ bhūtānāmātmani hotā bhūteṣu cātmānaṃ ; tathā hi vijñāyate ; hutāhaṃ bhūteśvātmānaṃ juhavāni bhūtāni cātmani |*) (Śa. Brā. 13-7-1-1)

The sentence of the Śatapatha Brāhmaṇa tells the meaning of this epithet: because he has himself entered all worlds and all classes of beings and has absorbed all of them in himself, the epithet Bhauvana is well known for Viśvakarman. As he is the lord of all the worlds, he is Indra; as he pervades the three worlds, he is Agni; such praises too are many. That Indra himself fulfilled his task in the form of Viśvakarman is shown by an historical occasion told in the Aitareya Brāhmaṇa. On this occasion the identity of Prajāpati, the creator of the whole world, with Viśvakarman is shown. How? Thus:

इंद्रो वै वृत्रं हत्वा विश्वकर्माभवत्प्रजापतिः प्रजाः सृष्ट्वा विश्वकर्माभवत्संवत्सरो विश्वकर्मेंद्रमेव तदात्मानं प्रजापतिं संवत्सरं विश्वकर्माणमाप्नुवंतींद्र एव तदात्मनि प्रजापतौ संवत्सरे विश्वकर्मण्यं ततः प्रतितिष्ठंति .... .... ॥ (*iṃdro vai vṛtraṃ hatvā viśvakarmābhavatprajāpatiḥ prajāḥ sṛṣṭvā viśvakarmābhavatsaṃvatsaro viśvakarmeṃdrameva tadātmānaṃ prajāpatiṃ saṃvatsaraṃ viśvakarmāṇamāpnuvaṃtīṃdra eva tadātmani prajāpatau saṃvatsare viśvakarmaṇyaṃ tataḥ pratitiṣṭhaṃti .... .... ‖*) (Ai. Brā. 4-22)

Indra killed Vṛtra and obtained the form of Viśvakarman. Prajāpati, having created the creatures, became Viśvakarman. Viśvakarman is of the nature of the year. Indra too is of the nature of the year. Therefore, by contemplation of Indra, the realisation of the year, Prajāpati and Viśvakarman is obtained. In the end, the refuge of these protects the sacrificer. It is declared that one who knows this principle has a firm refuge without doubt. This Viśvakarman is Prajāpati himself, the source of the whole world.

### Printed p. 171

रसो ना ऊर्मिर्विश्वकर्मा त ऋषिरिति प्रजापतिर्वै विश्वकर्मा प्रजापतिसृष्टासि (*raso nā ūrmirviśvakarmā ta ṛṣiriti prajāpatirvai viśvakarmā prajāpatisṛṣṭāsi*) (Śa. Brā. 8 2-1-10) **GLOSS** **NOTE**

विष्टंभो वय इति । प्रजापतिर्वै विष्टंभः स वयोऽभवदधिपतिश्चंद इति प्रजापतिर्वा अधिपतिः प्रजापतिरेवच्छंदोऽभवत् । विश्वकर्मा वय इति । प्रजापतिर्वै विश्वकर्मा स वयोऽभवत् ॥ (*viṣṭaṃbho vaya iti | prajāpatirvai viṣṭaṃbhaḥ sa vayo 'bhavadadhipatiścaṃda iti prajāpatirvā adhipatiḥ prajāpatirevacchaṃdo 'bhavat | viśvakarmā vaya iti | prajāpatirvai viśvakarmā sa vayo 'bhavat ‖*) (Śa. Brā. 8-2-3-12) **GLOSS**

प्रजापतिर्नै विश्वकर्मेति । प्रजापतिर्नै विश्वकर्मा । स हीदग्ं सर्वमकरोन्मनो गंधर्वस्तस्य ऋक्सामान्यप्सरसः................ (*prajāpatirnai viśvakarmeti | prajāpatirnai viśvakarmā | sa hīdagṃ sarvamakaronmano gaṃdharvastasya ṛksāmānyapsarasaḥ................*) (Śa. Brā. 9-4-1-12) **GLOSS**

In these and similar sentences the identity of Prajāpati and Viśvakarman is shown. The sacrifice, which is the navel of the world, has Prajāpati in the form of Viśvakarman as its support (refuge). So it comes to this: Viśvakarman is the refuge of the sacrifice, which is the basis of the world's activity. This support is itself of the nature of power (śakti). (Without power there is no support.) Therefore Prajāpati, the support of the principle of sacrifice, is himself this power. Because Prajāpati makes everything, he has the name Viśvakarman. In this way the nature of Viśvakarman is praised. The natures of those deities whom the Taittirīya Brāhmaṇa describes as विश्वसृजः (*viśvasṛjaḥ*) are all contained in Viśvakarman (Tai. Brā. 3-12-9). This account will be given in the Special Topics of the Ṛk beginning विश्वतश्चक्षुः (*viśvataścakṣuḥ*).
